# Nepal seed list for search-engine-scraper.
#
# Built on 2026-09-23 from public crt.sh certificate-transparency data and
# official DOIB registered-online-media data, following discovery guidance in
# seeds/README_SIR.md. Registry inclusion or certificate presence does not
# guarantee that a host is live or permits crawling. Runtime must respect
# robots.txt, per-host rate limits, and crawl policies.
#
# Format: [category priority], followed by one HTTPS seed URL per line.
# Higher priorities run first. Discovered links inherit their seed priority.
# Major public institutions, courts, finance, media/news, government,
# education, and military sites appear first. Registrable domains precede
# subdomains.
# cPanel service hosts (mail/email variants, webmail, webdisk, cPanel, WHM,
# FTP, discovery, calendar, and contact hosts) collapse to their registrable
# .np domain.
# Media source: https://giwmscdnone.gov.np/media/pdf_upload/
# online%20reg%202073-2083_w5ycxjq.pdf
# Unique valid seed URLs: 12094
#
# Run with:
#   ./bin/scraper --seeds-file=my_part/seeds.example.txt --same-host-only=true
